# Council of boards: the empty halyard of the flagship. Every option writes
# NAM_board_expedition_flag, read only by the island concession tenders 125-127.
# Fired by the first island launch, or by operation Poseidon when no island was
# launched; in the war of return every option then dispatches event 124.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.101
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.101.t
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.101.d.leaders
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.101.d
		trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed }
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.101.a
		add_political_power = -20
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_101_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 2 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			set_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_war_of_return_active = yes }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.124 days = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.101.b
		army_experience = 15
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_101_b_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_101_b_leaders_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# Olshansky (chair 0) has no company; the council keeps the fee instead.
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_board_chair > 0 }
					check_variable = { NAM_board_chair < 7 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_chair_gain value = 8 }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = 40 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_holders_gain value = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_others_gain value = 0 }
				NAM_board_holders_package = yes
			}
			else = {
				# The first island launch can come before the partition night: the
				# two current vote leaders pay instead of the holders.
				NAM_board_recount = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_leader_a value = NAM_board_order^0 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_leader_b value = NAM_board_order^1 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { NAM_board_chair_gain > 0 } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_chair }
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = NAM_board_chair_gain }
					NAM_board_support_change = yes
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_chair_gain value = 0 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_leader_a }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_leader_b }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_recount = yes
			}
			set_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 2 }
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_war_of_return_active = yes }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.124 days = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.101.c
		add_stability = 0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_101_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = -2 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			set_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 3 }
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_war_of_return_active = yes }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.124 days = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# Invasion preparation, war economy lane: the charter, the cartel and tender pair and the capstone.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.102
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.102.t
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.102.d.leaders
		trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.102.d
		trigger = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed }
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_ferrant_steel
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.102.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_102_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -30 }
			NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 3 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.102.b
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 3500
			producer = NAM
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_102_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = -5 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.102.c
		add_manpower = 2500
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_102_c_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_102_c_leaders_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed }
				# Smenkom's gain goes in first: the holders package recounts once for both,
				# and a holder that is Smenkom receives both rows.
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 5 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_holders_gain value = -3 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_others_gain value = 0 }
				NAM_board_holders_package = yes
			}
			else = {
				# The charter can be signed before the partition night: the two
				# current vote leaders pay instead of the holders, ranked before
				# Smenkom's gain; a leader that is Smenkom receives both rows.
				NAM_board_recount = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_leader_a value = NAM_board_order^0 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_leader_b value = NAM_board_order^1 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 5 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_leader_a }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_leader_b }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_recount = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.118.a
		add_political_power = -15
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_118_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			67 = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = 5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			688 = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = 5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			689 = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = 5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			690 = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = 5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			700 = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = 5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			719 = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = 5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.118.b
		add_political_power = 30
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_118_b_leaders_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# Fired before the partition night, when no holders exist yet: the
			# list goes to the two current vote leaders, the same loop as the night.
			NAM_board_recount = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_story_index value = -1 }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = 0
				end = 6
				value = NAM_board_loop_step
				add_to_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_story_index value = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_order^NAM_board_story_index }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -2 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { NAM_board_story_index < 2 } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 6 }
				}
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
			}
			NAM_board_recount = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.118.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_118_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			688 = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = 10 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			700 = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = 10 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = -3 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_support^6 value = 45 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Island operations: the Ulven concession tender, Navis against Ferrant. Fired one day
# after NAM_isl_settle_victory; a postponement fires it again after 90 days, so the
# done flag replaces fire_only_once and the trigger drops a stale repeat.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.125
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = NAM_isl_tender_done_1 }
		648 = { is_owned_by = NAM }
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_isl_owner^1 value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.a
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 1 compare = equals } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_125_a_waived_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_125_a_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# Both bidders' loss (waived under the council's flag) and the repeat in 90 days.
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_isl_k value = 1 }
			NAM_isl_tender_postpone = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_125_b_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_chair value = 3 compare = equals }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_isl_tender_chair_bonus_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# The operator is written before the recount, so the concession votes count at once.
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_isl_k value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 3 }
			NAM_isl_tender_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_support^3 value = NAM_board_support^2 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.125.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_125_c_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_expedition_flag value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_chair value = 2 compare = equals }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_isl_tender_chair_bonus_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			# The operator is written before the recount, so the concession votes count at once.
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_isl_k value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 2 }
			NAM_isl_tender_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_support^2 value = NAM_board_support^3 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
	}
}

			NAM_isl_flare_settle = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.129.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_129_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_isl_flare_answer value = 3 }
			NAM_isl_flare_settle = yes

# Council of boards: an exposed safehouse in a mainland district. Fired by the
# monthly cell pulse or by the enemy's raid; the district is the event target
# NAM_board_cells_district, saved by the caller.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.130
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		NOT = { NAM_war_of_return_active = yes }
		event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
			OR = {
				is_owned_by = EFL
				is_owned_by = AZH
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.a
		add_political_power = -15
		add_manpower = 1000
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_130_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = -5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			clr_country_flag = NAM_board_cells_event_pending
			clear_global_event_target = NAM_board_cells_district
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_130_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# The same cooldown a raid sets: no raid and no exposure roll there.
			event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = -15 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
				set_state_flag = { flag = NAM_cells_raided days = 120 value = 1 }
			}
			clr_country_flag = NAM_board_cells_event_pending
			clear_global_event_target = NAM_board_cells_district
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.130.c
		event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
			owner = {
				add_stability = -0.02
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_130_c_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_130_c_leaders_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_cells_change value = -5 }
				NAM_board_cells_add = yes
			}
			# The district's holder is the partition holder of its owner. Before
			# the night the company the night would give it pays: the vote leader
			# for Eflor, the runner-up for Azhar, as partition_night assigns them.
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_board_partition_fixed }
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = { is_owned_by = EFL }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_partition_efl }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_partition_azh }
				}
			}
			else = {
				NAM_board_recount = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:NAM_board_cells_district = { is_owned_by = EFL }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_rank_1 }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_rank_2 }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -4 }
			NAM_board_support_change = yes
			NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			clr_country_flag = NAM_board_cells_event_pending
			clear_global_event_target = NAM_board_cells_district
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# War of return: the council's armistice offer reaches the first principal that
# can still decide. A human Eflor or Azhar chooses; the AI usually refuses while
# it holds an island. A principal beaten in the day of delivery never answers.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.133
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.133.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.133.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_administrative_office
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		NAM_rtw_can_decide = yes
		NAM_war_of_return_active = yes
		NAM = {
			check_variable = { var = NAM_board_armistice_terms value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.133.a
		add_war_support = -0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_133_a_tt
		# The holders' terms cede the claimed belt land NAM controls; the council's
		# terms drop the claims.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NAM = {
					check_variable = { NAM_board_armistice_terms > 1 }
					NAM_board_belt_controlled = yes
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_133_a_belt_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NAM = {
					check_variable = { NAM_board_armistice_terms < 2 }
					NAM_board_belt_claimed = yes
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_133_a_claims_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			save_event_target_as = NAM_board_armistice_acceptor
			NAM = { NAM_board_poseidon_armistice = yes }
		}
		# Against the refusal's weight 20: 80% acceptance, 20% while the
		# coalition holds a Directory island (5 against 20).
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.0625
				OR = {
					225 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					226 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					227 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					228 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					229 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					230 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
					231 = {
						OR = {
							is_controlled_by = EFL
							is_controlled_by = AZH
						}
					}
				}
			}
			# The quarrel over the spoils makes the leader readier to settle;
			# the holders' terms that keep occupied belt land make it less so.
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_idea = NAM_sub_trophy_quarrel
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NAM = {
					check_variable = { NAM_board_armistice_terms > 1 }
					NAM_board_belt_controlled = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.133.b
		add_political_power = -25
		add_war_support = 0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_133_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.134 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

# ----- Council of boards: subversive operations -----
# The council hears of an exposed operation. Fired by NAM_sub_expose, which
# writes NAM_sub_exposed_target (1 Eflor, 2 Azhar) and NAM_sub_exposed_runner
# (the company blamed, 0 when no company chairs a council operation) only when
# no hearing is pending; every answer clears both.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.135
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.135.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.135.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		check_variable = { NAM_sub_exposed_target > 0 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.135.a
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { NAM_sub_exposed_runner > 0 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_135_a_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_sub_exposed_runner }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = -15
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_135_a_nochair_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = { NAM_sub_exposed_clear = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.135.b
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { NAM_sub_exposed_runner > 0 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_135_b_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -40 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_sub_exposed_runner }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_135_b_nochair_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -40 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { NAM_sub_exposed_clear = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.135.c
		add_war_support = 0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_135_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_sub_exposed_target = 1 } }
				EFL = {
					set_country_flag = {
						flag = NAM_sub_vigilant
						value = 1
						days = 180
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				AZH = {
					set_country_flag = {
						flag = NAM_sub_vigilant
						value = 1
						days = 180
					}
				}
			}
			NAM_sub_exposed_clear = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

				NAM_board_support_change = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_board_partition_azh > 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_partition_azh }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 3 }
			NAM_board_support_change = yes
			NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.139.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_139_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_rtw_bonds value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_holders_gain value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_others_gain value = 0 }
			NAM_board_holders_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# War of return: a lawyer brings a writ against the emergency powers. Fired by
# NAM_rtw_chairmans_war. NAM_rtw_find_boycott leaves NAM_rtw_boycott_target
# (the lowest company index with stance -1, else 0); without a boycotting company
# the answers fall on Reidbank.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.140
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.140.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.140.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_parliament_chamber
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		NAM_war_of_return_active = yes
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_rtw_find_boycott = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.140.a
		add_political_power = -20
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { NAM_rtw_boycott_target > 0 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_140_a_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_rtw_boycott_target }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_140_a_bank_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = -3 }
				NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_variable = NAM_rtw_boycott_target
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.140.b

			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_140_b_tt

				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -40 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_rtw_boycott_target }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_140_b_bank_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -40 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 5 }
				NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_variable = NAM_rtw_boycott_target
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.140.c
		add_war_support = 0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_140_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = -2 }

# Eflor: the council demands its surrender (decision NAM_rtw_offer_surrender_efl).
# The refusal is the first option, so an unanswered demand lapses into a
# refusal and the escrow returns; acceptance settles once through
# NAM_rtw_accept_surrender_efl, which rechecks the war itself.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.157
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.157.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.157.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = EFL
		NAM_war_of_return_active = yes
		NAM = { has_country_flag = NAM_rtw_surrender_pending }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.157.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_157_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM = { NAM_rtw_surrender_refused = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.157.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				NAM = { NAM_rtw_direct_rule = yes }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_157_a_annex_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_157_a_district_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM = { NAM_rtw_accept_surrender_efl = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				add = 15
				has_idea = NAM_rtw_loren_crisis
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 30
				surrender_progress > 0.4
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				NAM = {
					controls_state = 67
					controls_state = 688
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 25
				has_idea = NAM_rtw_capital_fallen
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 25
				AZH = {
					exists = yes
					has_capitulated = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				NAM = { has_country_flag = NAM_rtw_surrender_bonus_efl }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -10
				NAM = { has_country_flag = NAM_rtw_surrender_malus_efl }
			}
			# No cabinet survives the council's direct rule.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NAM = { NAM_rtw_direct_rule = yes }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NAM = { surrender_progress > 0.3 }
				NOT = { surrender_progress > 0.2 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				OR = {
					225 = { is_controlled_by = EFL }
					226 = { is_controlled_by = EFL }
					227 = { is_controlled_by = EFL }
					228 = { is_controlled_by = EFL }
					229 = { is_controlled_by = EFL }
					230 = { is_controlled_by = EFL }
					231 = { is_controlled_by = EFL }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# The coalition's decider (NAM_rtw_pick_decider) chooses whether to dictate
# terms to the Directory. Fired monthly by NAM_rtw_dictate_check.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.158
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.158.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.158.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_military_planning
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			tag = EFL
			tag = AZH
		}
		NAM_war_of_return_active = yes
		NAM = { has_country_flag = NAM_rtw_dictate_pending }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.158.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_158_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.164 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			# Annexation is close: finish the Directory instead.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.3
				NAM = { surrender_progress > 0.45 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				surrender_progress > 0.3
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.158.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_158_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM = {
				clr_country_flag = NAM_rtw_dictate_pending
				set_country_flag = {
					flag = NAM_rtw_dictate_cooldown
					value = 1
					days = 90
				}
			}
			NAM_rtw_clear_decider = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

	}

# The Directory: the coalition dictates its terms (158 a). Accepting or buying
# the peace off is a lost pause; fighting on raises the next threshold and a
# second refusal ends the offers.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.164
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.164.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.164.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		NAM_war_of_return_active = yes
		has_country_flag = NAM_rtw_dictate_pending
		has_global_flag = NAM_rtw_decider_saved
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.164.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_164_a_tt
		effect_tooltip = {
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = NAM_rtw_reparations

# Council of boards, the continent: the treaty of concession reaches an emirate.
# Sent by event 214; FROM is the Directory. Answers a and b need the open offer
# and no great war in progress, so a late answer never makes a district during
# the war; answer c closes such an offer and returns the escrow.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.209
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.209.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.209.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.209.a
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = NAM_mdr_offer_pending
			NOT = { NAM_great_war_active = yes }
			FROM = { NAM_country_operable = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_209_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# The treaty has its own tender (event 210), so the signing skips
			# the settlement queue of event 212.
			NAM_mdr_desert_sign = yes
			FROM = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.210 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				strength_ratio = { tag = NAM ratio < 0.5 }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 15
				count_triggers = {
					amount = 2
					NAM = { is_neighbor_of = ROOT }
					EFL = {
						is_subject_of = NAM
						is_neighbor_of = ROOT
					}
					AZH = {
						is_subject_of = NAM
						is_neighbor_of = ROOT
					}
					KDR = {
						is_subject_of = NAM
						is_neighbor_of = ROOT
					}
					SDR = {
						is_subject_of = NAM
						is_neighbor_of = ROOT
					}
					RHM = {
						is_subject_of = NAM
						is_neighbor_of = ROOT
					}
					MZR = {
						is_subject_of = NAM
						is_neighbor_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 15
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { tag = KDR }
						KDR = { is_subject_of = NAM }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { tag = SDR }
						SDR = { is_subject_of = NAM }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { tag = RHM }
						RHM = { is_subject_of = NAM }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				NAM = { has_country_flag = NAM_mdr_council_seat_promised }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -30
				OR = {
					is_in_faction_with = SHL
					is_subject_of = SHL
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.209.b
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = NAM_mdr_offer_pending
			NOT = { NAM_great_war_active = yes }
			FROM = { NAM_country_operable = yes }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_209_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_mdr_desert_refuse = yes
			FROM = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.211 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.209.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = NAM_mdr_offer_pending
					NOT = { NAM_great_war_active = yes }
					FROM = { NAM_country_operable = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_209_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_mdr_desert_void = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
	}
}

# Council of boards, the continent: the tender queue of districts made by the
# great war's settlement or by a submission to the Directory. The district
# waits at index 0 until an answer removes it, so a district queued while the
# window is open never fires a second copy; the next entry follows a day later.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.212
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.212.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.212.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_smenkom_exchange
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		check_variable = { NAM_mdr_tender_queue^num > 0 }
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = NAM_mdr_tender_target value = NAM_mdr_tender_queue^0 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.212.k1
		trigger = {
			var:NAM_mdr_tender_target = {
				tag = KDR
				NAM_board_federal_subject = yes
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_tender_k1_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			var:NAM_mdr_tender_target = {
				set_variable = { var = NAM_corp_district_owner value = 1 }
				NAM_corp_district_apply = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 8 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = -5 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			remove_from_array = {
				array = NAM_mdr_tender_queue
				index = 0
			}
			clear_variable = NAM_mdr_tender_target
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_mdr_tender_queue^num > 0 } }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.212 days = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.212.k2
		trigger = {
			var:NAM_mdr_tender_target = {
				OR = {
					tag = SDR
					tag = MZR
				}

				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_mdr_tender_queue^num > 0 } }

			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
	}
	option = {

			var:NAM_mdr_tender_target = {
				tag = RHM

			}

# Council of boards, the continent: the tender for a leased state, fired by the
# completion of NAM_mdr_lease_land. The land decides the rival pair: Eflor's
# land Ferrant or Arktur, Azhar's land Loren or Reidbank. A state lost before
# the answer closes the lease without an owner.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.213
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.213.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.213.d

		has_variable = NAM_mdr_lease_pending
	}

		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.213.k1
		trigger = {
			var:NAM_mdr_lease_pending = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT

				NOT = { is_core_of = EFL }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_lease_k1_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_mdr_lease_company value = 1 }
			var:NAM_mdr_lease_pending = { NAM_mdr_lease_state = yes }

			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			clear_variable = NAM_mdr_lease_pending
			NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
		}

# Hidden. One day after NAM_mdr_desert_offer: every paid offer not yet sent goes
# to its emirate as event 209 while the emirate can still answer; otherwise its

	}

# Council of boards, the continent: a mainland lot goes to tender, fired by the
# completion of NAM_mdr_mainland_tender. The lot is the first still open
# (lot variable 0) in the cycle wells -> smelters -> yards that starts at
# NAM_mdr_lot_order (event 200); the NAM_mdr_tender_next_* triggers pick it. A
# won lot stores the company and answer c stores -1, so the next tender moves
# on.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.215
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		OR = {
			check_variable = { NAM_mdr_lot_wells = 0 }
			check_variable = { NAM_mdr_lot_smelters = 0 }
			check_variable = { NAM_mdr_lot_yards = 0 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.k1
		trigger = { NAM_mdr_tender_next_wells = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_lot_k1_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 0 }
			NAM_board_close_tender = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.k6
		trigger = { NAM_mdr_tender_next_wells = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_lot_k6_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 0 }
			NAM_board_close_tender = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.k2
		trigger = { NAM_mdr_tender_next_smelters = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_lot_k2_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 0 }
			NAM_board_close_tender = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.k5
		trigger = { NAM_mdr_tender_next_smelters = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_lot_k5_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 0 }
			NAM_board_close_tender = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.k3
		trigger = { NAM_mdr_tender_next_yards = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_lot_k3_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 0 }
			NAM_board_close_tender = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.k4
		trigger = { NAM_mdr_tender_next_yards = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_lot_k4_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 6 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 0 }
			NAM_board_close_tender = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.215.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_lot_public_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_mdr_tender_next_wells = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NAM_mdr_tender_next_smelters = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 5 }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_lot_input value = 6 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 0 }
			NAM_board_close_tender = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

# Council of boards, the continent: the desert question lapses, fired by
# NAM_mdr_desert_lapse_check on day 540 of the tree with no great war in
# progress. The open escrows are already refunded; this answer carries the
# price of the failure path.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.216
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.216.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.216.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_expedition_camp
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.216.a
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_war_support = -0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_216_a_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_mdr_offer_refund_tt
		hidden_effect = {

# Eflor's land: Dextung's officers meet in Middle Loren (eflor_province, 90-120 days
# later). Dropped when the Directory no longer holds any of Eflor's land.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.217
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.217.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.217.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_karamyshev_officers
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		any_of_scopes = {
			array = NAM_conquered_states
			is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
			is_core_of = EFL
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.217.a
		add_political_power = -40
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_217_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_mdr_compliance_add value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_mdr_core_filter value = 1 }
			NAM_mdr_conquered_compliance = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.217.b
		add_manpower = 6000
		add_stability = -0.03
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.217.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_217_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# The archive's lists halve the unrest the holder's own hunt provokes.
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = NAM_mdr_dextung_lists }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_mdr_resistance_add value = 5 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_mdr_core_filter value = 1 }
				NAM_mdr_conquered_resistance = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_mdr_resistance_add value = 10 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_mdr_core_filter value = 1 }
				NAM_mdr_conquered_resistance = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_board_partition_efl > 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_partition_efl }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = -4 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

# Partition of the South: lot 1, the Zarkhan belt. Fired by its private tender
# (decision NAM_ptn_tender_1), by the night's dispatcher (event 246) or by a
# re-tender of a kept lot (NAM_ptn_retender); each caller stores the mode in
# NAM_ptn_lot_mode^1 (1 tender, 2 night, 3 re-tender). Every answer goes through
# NAM_ptn_award with the stored mode; a night answer calls the dispatcher back.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.233
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.233.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.233.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_ferrant_steel
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		OR = {
			check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot_state^1 = 1 }
			check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot_state^1 = 3 }
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_input value = 1 }
			NAM_ptn_prepare_bids = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.233.a_mazar
		trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot_mode^1 = 3 } }
			NAM_hgm_mazar_ally = yes
			NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_shl_form = 1 } }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_233_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_input value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_company_input value = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_mode value = 0 }
			NAM_ptn_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.233.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot_mode^1 = 3 } }
			OR = {
				NAM_hgm_mazar_ally = no
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_shl_form = 1 }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_233_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_input value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_company_input value = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_mode value = 0 }
			NAM_ptn_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
	option = {
		name = NAM_ptn_lot_withdraw
		trigger = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot_mode^1 = 3 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ptn_lot_withdraw_tt

		}

		hidden_effect = {

			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_company_input value = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_mode value = 0 }
			NAM_ptn_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}

		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_242_b_tt
		if = {

			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ptn_grudge_tt

			NAM_ptn_award = yes
		}

			base = 40
			modifier = {
				add = 30
				check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lots_won^2 value = NAM_ptn_lots_won^5 compare = less_than }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				check_variable = { var = NAM_board_support^2 value = NAM_board_support^5 compare = less_than }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lots_won^2 > 1 }
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lots_won^5 < 2 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot_mode^10 = 2 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ptn_grudge_tt

			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_ptn_veto_tt
		}

# Dominion: the heads of the nine houses come to Khazar: board seats under the trust,

	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.248.t

		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.248.d_trust
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_shl_form = 1 } } }
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.248.d_inventory
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session

	fire_only_once = yes

	}

		add_political_power = -30
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_shl_form = 1 } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_248_a_trust_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_248_a_inventory_tt
		}

				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_shl_form = 1 } }

	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.251.a
		add_political_power = -40
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_251_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_story_company value = 0 }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = 1
				end = 12
				value = NAM_board_loop_step
				add_to_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_story_company value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^NAM_board_story_company value = 2 compare = equals } }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^NAM_board_story_company value = 1 compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^NAM_board_story_company value = 2 compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^NAM_board_story_company value = 3 compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^NAM_board_story_company value = 4 compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^NAM_board_story_company value = 5 compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^NAM_board_story_company value = 6 compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 2 }

			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 2 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {

			OR = {
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lots_won^1 > 0 }
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lots_won^2 > 0 }
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lots_won^3 > 0 }
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lots_won^4 > 0 }
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lots_won^5 > 0 }
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lots_won^6 > 0 }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_267_b_tt

			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = -2 }

			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = -2 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_pick_result = 1 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 6 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_pick_result = 2 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 6 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_pick_result = 3 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 6 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_pick_result = 4 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 6 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_pick_result = 5 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 6 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_ptn_pick_result = 6 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 6 }

# Dominion, final: the council becomes a league of districts. Fired by
# NAM_hgm_league_of_districts. "District owner" is the owner of an operating district of
# NAM_federal_districts at the answer.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.268
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.268.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.268.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_loren_oil
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.268.a
		add_political_power = -50
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_268_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = NAM_federal_districts
				if = {
					limit = {
						NAM_board_operating_district = yes
						has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 1 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 2 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 3 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 4 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 5 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 2 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 6 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = 2 }
					}
				}
			}

		}

	}

				array = NAM_federal_districts

					limit = {
						NAM_board_operating_district = yes
						has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 1 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 5 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 2 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = 5 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 3 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 5 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { NAM_corp_district_owner = 4 } }
						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = 5 }
					}
					if = {

						set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = 5 }
					}
					if = {

					}

# Dominion: the letter to the debtor lacks one line - the Directory's demand. Fired by
# NAM_hgm_hegemons_price after NAM_hgm_pick_debtor; dropped when no debtor was found
# (the focus then paid 150 capital). Each answer sets the demand and sends the letter
# (event 277 to the debtor) through NAM_hgm_send_price.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.276
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.276.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.276.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		has_variable = NAM_hgm_price_debtor
		var:NAM_hgm_price_debtor = { exists = yes }
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			var:NAM_hgm_price_debtor = { save_event_target_as = NAM_hgm_price_debtor }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.276.a
		add_political_power = -30
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_276_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_price_demand value = 1 }
			NAM_hgm_send_price = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.276.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_276_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_price_demand value = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = -3 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			NAM_hgm_send_price = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.276.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_276_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_price_demand value = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = -3 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
			NAM_hgm_send_price = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }

# Dominion: the debtor answers the hegemon's letter. ROOT is the debtor; NAM keeps the
# demand in NAM_hgm_price_demand. Its AI accepts while its opinion of NAM is above 25 or
# its war with RUS goes badly. Acceptance starts the year of tribute that
# NAM_hgm_loans_monthly pays; refusal forfeits the open credit.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.277
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.277.t
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.277.d
		trigger = { NAM = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_price_demand < 2 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.277.d_harbour
		trigger = { NAM = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_price_demand = 2 } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.277.d_concession
		trigger = { NAM = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_price_demand > 2 } } }
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_reidbank_credit
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		exists = yes
		NOT = { has_war_with = NAM }
		NAM = {
			has_variable = NAM_hgm_price_demand
			has_variable = NAM_hgm_price_debtor
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.277.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_277_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			give_military_access = NAM
			NAM = {
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_tribute_until value = global.num_days }
				add_to_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_tribute_until value = 365 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_price_demand = 2 } }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
						type = convoy_1
						amount = 10
						producer = NAM
					}
					set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = 5 }
					NAM_board_trust_package = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				add = 99
				OR = {
					has_opinion = {
						target = NAM
						value > 25
					}
					AND = {
						has_war_with = RUS
						surrender_progress > 0.1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.277.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_277_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = NAM
				modifier = NAM_hgm_price_refused
			}
			NAM = { NAM_hgm_forfeit_credit = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_opinion = {
						target = NAM
						value > 25
					}
					AND = {
						has_war_with = RUS
						surrender_progress > 0.1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Dominion: the founders' heirs wait for their bench.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.278
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.278.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.278.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.278.a
		add_political_power = -30
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_278_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# Proxy votes expire at the next annual meeting, which resets the rows.
			NAM_hgm_heirs_spread = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.278.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_278_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# The heirs' votes are permanent; NAM_board_recount adds them to the
			# company's extra count. +2 here and -2 in the package keep the chair's
			# company's trust unchanged.
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_board_chair > 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_heir_votes^NAM_board_chair value = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_board_chair }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 2 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_1 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_2 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_3 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_4 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_5 value = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_trust_input_6 value = -2 }
			NAM_board_trust_package = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.278.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_278_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_first_input value = NAM_board_rank_1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_last_input value = NAM_board_rank_6 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_last_input > 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_heir_votes^NAM_hgm_last_input value = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_hgm_last_input }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_first_input > 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_hgm_first_input }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
			}
			NAM_board_recount = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
}

# Dominion: Reidbank's auditors set the richest board against the poorest.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.279
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.279.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.279.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_reidbank_credit
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			# The richest company by the recount's holdings; ties go to the lower number.
			set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_rich value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_top value = NAM_board_districts_1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_2 value = NAM_hgm_count_top compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_rich value = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_top value = NAM_board_districts_2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_3 value = NAM_hgm_count_top compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_rich value = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_top value = NAM_board_districts_3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_4 value = NAM_hgm_count_top compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_rich value = 4 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_top value = NAM_board_districts_4 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_5 value = NAM_hgm_count_top compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_rich value = 5 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_top value = NAM_board_districts_5 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_6 value = NAM_hgm_count_top compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_rich value = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_top value = NAM_board_districts_6 }
			}
			# The poorest of the other five; ties go to the lower number.
			set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_poor value = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_rich = 1 } }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_poor = 0 }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_1 value = NAM_hgm_count_low compare = less_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_poor value = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_low value = NAM_board_districts_1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_rich = 2 } }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_poor = 0 }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_2 value = NAM_hgm_count_low compare = less_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_poor value = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_low value = NAM_board_districts_2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_rich = 3 } }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_poor = 0 }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_3 value = NAM_hgm_count_low compare = less_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_poor value = 3 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_low value = NAM_board_districts_3 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_rich = 4 } }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_poor = 0 }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_4 value = NAM_hgm_count_low compare = less_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_poor value = 4 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_low value = NAM_board_districts_4 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_rich = 5 } }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_poor = 0 }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_5 value = NAM_hgm_count_low compare = less_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_poor value = 5 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_low value = NAM_board_districts_5 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_rich = 6 } }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_poor = 0 }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_board_districts_6 value = NAM_hgm_count_low compare = less_than }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_poor value = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_low value = NAM_board_districts_6 }
			}
			# The richest company's lowest-index awarded lot (state 2). Fenn handed to
			# the Eflor district counts through Eflor's district row, so it never moves.
			set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_lot value = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_step value = 0 }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = 1
				end = 12
				value = NAM_hgm_count_loop
				add_to_temp_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_step value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot = 0 }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^NAM_hgm_count_step value = 2 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^NAM_hgm_count_step value = NAM_hgm_count_rich compare = equals }
						NOT = {
							AND = {
								check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_step = 8 }
								NOT = { country_exists = WEF }
								174 = { is_owned_by = EFL }
							}
						}
					}
					set_variable = { var = NAM_hgm_count_lot value = NAM_hgm_count_step }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.279.a
		add_political_power = -30
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_279_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_hgm_count_poor }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -3 }
			NAM_board_support_change = yes
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_rich
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_poor
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_lot
			NAM_board_recount = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.279.b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot > 0 }
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = NAM_hgm_perpetual_council }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_279_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^NAM_hgm_count_lot value = NAM_hgm_count_poor }
			# A district lot also changes its concession; the district keeps its
			# NAM_hgm_owner_fixed flag, because the council moved it, not a dispute.
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot = 5 }
					country_exists = KYZ
					KYZ = { has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner }
				}
				KYZ = {
					set_variable = { var = NAM_corp_district_owner value = NAM.NAM_hgm_count_poor }
					NAM_corp_district_apply = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot = 6 }
					country_exists = GLP
					GLP = { has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner }
				}
				GLP = {
					set_variable = { var = NAM_corp_district_owner value = NAM.NAM_hgm_count_poor }
					NAM_corp_district_apply = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot = 7 }
					country_exists = MZR
					MZR = { has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner }
				}
				MZR = {
					set_variable = { var = NAM_corp_district_owner value = NAM.NAM_hgm_count_poor }
					NAM_corp_district_apply = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot = 8 }
					country_exists = WEF
					WEF = { has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner }
				}
				WEF = {
					set_variable = { var = NAM_corp_district_owner value = NAM.NAM_hgm_count_poor }
					NAM_corp_district_apply = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot = 9 }
					country_exists = KDR
					KDR = { has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner }
				}
				KDR = {
					set_variable = { var = NAM_corp_district_owner value = NAM.NAM_hgm_count_poor }
					NAM_corp_district_apply = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot = 10 }
					country_exists = SDR
					SDR = { has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner }
				}
				SDR = {
					set_variable = { var = NAM_corp_district_owner value = NAM.NAM_hgm_count_poor }
					NAM_corp_district_apply = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { NAM_hgm_count_lot = 11 }
					country_exists = RHM
					RHM = { has_variable = NAM_corp_district_owner }
				}
				RHM = {
					set_variable = { var = NAM_corp_district_owner value = NAM.NAM_hgm_count_poor }
					NAM_corp_district_apply = yes
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_grievance^NAM_hgm_count_rich value = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_hgm_count_rich }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -8 }
			NAM_board_support_change = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_hgm_count_poor }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 6 }
			NAM_board_support_change = yes
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_rich
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_poor
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_lot
			NAM_board_recount = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.279.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_279_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = 150 }
			NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_hgm_count_rich }
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -5 }
			NAM_board_support_change = yes
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_rich
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_poor
			clear_variable = NAM_hgm_count_lot
			NAM_board_recount = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

# Dominion: two lot holders' surveyors meet at one boundary stone (southern_cadastre).
# The winner's lots pay the cadastre bonus through NAM_ptn_monthly for good.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.280
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.280.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.280.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_smenkom_exchange
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			# The two companies with the most lots of this partition dispute the boundary
			# (NAM_ptn_cadastre_first and _second, read by NAMPtnCadastreFirst and
			# NAMPtnCadastreSecond); with fewer than two lot holders the first two by votes.
			NAM_ptn_cadastre_pick = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.280.a
		add_political_power = -30
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.280.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_280_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_cadastre_winner value = 1 }
			NAM_ptn_cadastre_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.280.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_280_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_cadastre_winner value = 2 }
			NAM_ptn_cadastre_award = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

# Dominion: the people of country lot 5 answer the partition: Keyzan's highland princes

country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.281
	title = {
		trigger = {
			country_exists = KYZ
			KYZ = {
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.281.t_district
	}
	title = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					country_exists = KYZ
					KYZ = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
					}
				}
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.281.t_land
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			country_exists = KYZ
			KYZ = {
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.281.d_district
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					country_exists = KYZ
					KYZ = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
					}
				}
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.281.d_land
	}
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_reidbank_credit
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		OR = {
			check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^5 value = 2 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^5 value = 3 compare = equals }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				country_exists = KYZ
				KYZ = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
				}
			}
			any_of_scopes = {
				array = NAM_conquered_states
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot = 5 }
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			# NAMPtnLotOwner names the company of this lot, or the council for a kept lot.
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_input value = 5 }
			NAM_ptn_focus_lot = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.281.a
		add_political_power = -30
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KYZ
				KYZ = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_281_a_district_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_281_a_land_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = KYZ
					KYZ = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
					}
				}
				KYZ = { add_stability = -0.05 }
			}
			else = {
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_conquered_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
							check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot = 5 }
						}
						add_resistance = 5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.281.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KYZ
				KYZ = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^5 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_281_b_kept_district_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_281_b_district_tt
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^5 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_281_b_kept_land_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_281_b_land_tt
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^5 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -60 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^5 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = KYZ
					KYZ = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
					}
				}
				KYZ = { add_stability = 0.05 }
			}
			else = {
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_conquered_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
							check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot = 5 }
						}
						add_compliance = 10
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.281.c
		if = {
			limit = {

				KYZ = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
				}
			}
			if = {

				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^5 value = 3 compare = equals } } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^5 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes

						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT

# Dominion: the people of country lot 6 answer the partition: Veyr's caravan guilds and
# their court. Fired by NAM_ptn_apply_lot 60-120 days after the lot is applied; district
# or land by the lot's form, the owner company or the council's purse for a kept lot.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.282
	title = {
		trigger = {
			country_exists = GLP
			GLP = {
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
			}
		}
		text = ADISCORD_nam_story.282.t_district
	}
	title = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					country_exists = GLP
					GLP = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
					}
				}
			}

				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_284_b_kept_district_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_284_b_district_tt
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^8 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_284_b_kept_land_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_284_b_land_tt
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^8 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -60 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^8 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							country_exists = WEF
							WEF = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
								has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
							}
						}
						AND = {
							NOT = { country_exists = WEF }
							check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^8 value = 2 compare = equals }
							country_exists = EFL
							EFL = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
								has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
							}
							OR = {
								174 = { is_owned_by = EFL }
								175 = { is_owned_by = EFL }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { country_exists = WEF }
					WEF = { add_stability = 0.05 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { country_exists = EFL }
					EFL = { add_stability = 0.05 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_conquered_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
							check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot = 8 }
						}
						add_compliance = 10
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
