		has_country_flag = NAM_board_opened
		check_variable = { var = NAM_board_ended value = 0 compare = equals }
		OR = {
			check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^11 value = 2 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^11 value = 3 compare = equals }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				country_exists = RHM
				RHM = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
				}
			}
			any_of_scopes = {
				array = NAM_conquered_states
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot = 11 }
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			# NAMPtnLotOwner names the company of this lot, or the council for a kept lot.
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_input value = 11 }
			NAM_ptn_focus_lot = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.287.a
		add_political_power = -30
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = RHM
				RHM = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_a_district_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_a_land_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = RHM
					RHM = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
					}
				}
				RHM = { add_stability = -0.05 }
			}
			else = {
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_conquered_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
							check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot = 11 }
						}
						add_resistance = 5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.287.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = RHM
				RHM = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^11 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_b_kept_district_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_b_district_tt
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^11 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_b_kept_land_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_b_land_tt
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^11 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -60 }
				NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^11 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = -5 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = RHM
					RHM = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
					}
				}
				RHM = { add_stability = 0.05 }
			}
			else = {
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_conquered_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
							check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot = 11 }
						}
						add_compliance = 10
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.287.c
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = RHM
				RHM = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
					has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^11 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_c_kept_district_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_c_district_tt
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^11 value = 3 compare = equals } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_c_kept_land_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_287_c_land_tt
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_corp_capital_change value = -60 }
			NAM_corp_capital_add = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = NAM_ptn_lot_state^11 value = 3 compare = equals } } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_company_input value = NAM_ptn_lot_owner^11 }
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_board_delta_input value = 3 }
				NAM_board_support_change = yes
				NAM_board_queue_recount = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					country_exists = RHM
					RHM = {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
						has_country_flag = NAM_federal_district
					}
				}
				RHM = { add_stability = 0.03 }
			}
			else = {
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_conquered_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
							check_variable = { NAM_ptn_lot = 11 }
						}
						add_compliance = 5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
	}
}

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						character = NAM_Egor_Rom
						ruling_only = yes
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 1 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 2 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.40 days = 30 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.327.b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		add_stability = 0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_327_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 200 }
			NAM_spend_treasury = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 5 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						character = NAM_Egor_Rom
						ruling_only = yes
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 1 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 2 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.40 days = 30 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.327.c
		add_war_support = 0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_327_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_garr_support value = 8 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = -5 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						character = NAM_Egor_Rom
						ruling_only = yes
					}
					OR = {

# pw_council_of_seals: whose signature opens the council's minutes.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.330
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.330.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.330.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { has_capitulated = no }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.330.a
		add_political_power = -50
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_330_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# The unsigned minutes pull the balance 5 points toward the centre;
			# inside -0.05..0.05 it stays where it is.
			if = {
				limit = { has_power_balance = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop } }
				if = {
					limit = { power_balance_value = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop value < -0.05 } }
					add_power_balance_value = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop value = 0.05 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { power_balance_value = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop value > 0.05 } }
					add_power_balance_value = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop value = -0.05 }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.330.b
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_power_balance = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop } } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop value = -0.10 }
		}
		add_command_power = 25
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_330_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_garr_support value = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = -3 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.330.c
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_power_balance = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop } } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = NAM_postwar_future_bop value = 0.10 }
		}
		add_stability = 0.03
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_330_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_nam_support value = -3 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# balanced_accounts: the treasury opens its books and stands surety. Every answer
# queues Rom's last note while he rules after a compact ending.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.349
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.349.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.349.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_board_session
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { has_capitulated = no }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.349.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_349_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 400 }
			NAM_spend_treasury = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_nam_support value = 3 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_oil_support value = 3 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 3 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_garr_support value = 3 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_trade_support value = 3 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						character = NAM_Egor_Rom
						ruling_only = yes
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 5 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 6 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.40 days = 30 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.349.b
		add_political_power = 40
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_349_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_trade_support value = 8 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = -5 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						character = NAM_Egor_Rom
						ruling_only = yes
					}
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 5 compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = NAM_prog_ending value = 6 compare = equals }
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_story.40 days = 30 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.349.c

		add_war_support = 0.05

# prag_settlement_agreements: the war-time oil accounts that differ by a train.
# Contest rows act only while the contest for the South is open.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.354
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.354.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.354.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { has_capitulated = no }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.354.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_354_a_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				hidden_trigger = { ADISCORD_south_contest_open = yes }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_354_a_south_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 200 }
			NAM_spend_treasury = yes
			if = {
				limit = { ADISCORD_south_contest_open = yes }
				NAM_prag_collect_contest_countries = yes
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_prag_contest_countries
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_client }
						set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_south_push_delta value = 4 }
						ADISCORD_south_push_influence_north = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.354.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_354_b_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				hidden_trigger = { ADISCORD_south_contest_open = yes }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_354_b_south_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 300 }
			NAM_add_treasury = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_trade_support value = 4 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
			if = {
				limit = { ADISCORD_south_contest_open = yes }
				NAM_prag_collect_contest_countries = yes
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_prag_contest_countries
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = NAM_oil_client }
						set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_south_push_delta value = 4 }
						ADISCORD_south_push_influence_shl = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {

						set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_south_push_delta value = 4 }
						ADISCORD_south_push_influence_shl = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

# prag_credit_supplies: whose wagons return north while the cargo is unpaid.
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.357
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.357.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.357.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_negotiation_table
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { has_capitulated = no }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.357.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_357_a_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				hidden_trigger = { ADISCORD_south_contest_open = yes }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_357_a_south_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 300 }
			NAM_spend_treasury = yes
			if = {
				limit = { ADISCORD_south_contest_open = yes }
				NAM_prag_collect_contest_countries = yes
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = NAM_prag_contest_countries
					if = {
						limit = { ADISCORD_south_lean_north_20 = yes }
						set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_south_push_delta value = 4 }
						ADISCORD_south_push_influence_north = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.357.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_357_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 300 }
			NAM_add_treasury = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_trade_support value = -5 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.357.c
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment
			amount = 100
			producer = NAM
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_357_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_garr_support value = 4 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_trade_support value = -3 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

	id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.450
	title = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.450.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.450.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_nam_weber_committees
	is_triggered_only = yes
	timeout_days = 7
	trigger = {
		NAM_board_operable = yes
		has_idea = NAM_judas_weber_committees
		has_variable = NAM_judas_weber_pending
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.450.a
		ai_chance = { base = 15 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_weber_ignore_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_judas_weber_ignore = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.450.b
		trigger = {
			NAM_judas_weber_can_concede = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = NAM_board_subsidised_opposition }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_weber_concede_paid_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_weber_concede_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_judas_weber_concede = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.450.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 85
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { NAM_judas_willing_delegate = yes }
				NOT = { NAM_judas_willing_arktur = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NAM_judas_willing_delegate = yes
					NAM_judas_willing_arktur = yes
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_weber_force_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_weber_force_reason
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NAM_judas_willing_delegate = yes
						NAM_judas_willing_arktur = yes
					}
				}
				NAM_judas_weber_force = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_board_operable = yes
					has_idea = NAM_judas_weber_committees
					has_variable = NAM_judas_weber_pending
				}
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.450 }
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = NAM_board_hereditary_roll }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_karamyshev_concede_roll_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_karamyshev_concede_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			NAM_judas_karamyshev_concede = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.451.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 85
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { NAM_judas_karamyshev_can_force = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = NAM_board_old_settlers_oath
				has_country_flag = NAM_judas_receipts_published
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_karamyshev_force_oath_receipts_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = NAM_board_old_settlers_oath }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_karamyshev_force_oath_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NAM_judas_karamyshev_can_force = yes }
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_karamyshev_force_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_judas_karamyshev_force_reason
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NAM_judas_karamyshev_can_force = yes }
				NAM_judas_karamyshev_force = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NAM_board_operable = yes
					has_idea = NAM_judas_karamyshev_watch
					has_variable = NAM_judas_karamyshev_pending
				}
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war.451 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# ----- Post-war republic: shared branches and the heritage tree -----
# Focus rewards of the shared post-war branches and of the heritage root fire
# these scenes from the last line of their hidden_effect; the heritage lanes of
# each course fire the events of that course's section. The first option is
# the one an unattended event takes on timeout; it always costs something.

# Shared: the artillery train reaches Sukhorechye before the bridge is ready (pw_breakthrough_army).
country_event = {
	id = ADISCORD_nam_story.301
	title = ADISCORD_nam_story.301.t
	desc = ADISCORD_nam_story.301.d
	picture = GFX_event_adiscord_rail_yard_waybills
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { has_capitulated = no }
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.301.a
		army_experience = 15
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_301_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 150 }
			NAM_spend_treasury = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.301.b
		add_political_power = -40
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = artillery_equipment
			amount = 36
			producer = NAM
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = ADISCORD_nam_story.301.c
		army_experience = -10
		690 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_by = ROOT
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					infrastructure < 5
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = NAM_story_301_c_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						690 = {
							is_owned_by = ROOT
							is_controlled_by = ROOT
							infrastructure < 5
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { var = NAM_treasury_amount value = 150 }
				NAM_add_treasury = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_dist_support value = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { var = NAM_grp_garr_support value = -5 }
			NAM_groups_refresh = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}
